Generative Learning algorithms ^To5UAeYu

Using binary classification as an example, and the following dataset: ^vekB9ypA

What logistic regression would do is search for a decision boundary that separates the positive and negative examples.  ^XjcG2914

There's a different class of algorithms that don't try to search for that separation, the generative learning algorithms. 
Rather than look at two classes and trying to find the separation, instead, it's going to look at the classes one at a time and try to build a model of what that class looks like. ^WGbNKz8q
